行業回顧                                                         INDUSTRY REVIEW

1.   鋰資源市場分析                                                 1.   Analysis of lithium resource market

     全球鋰資源供給大部分來自鹽湖和硬岩                                            The majority of global lithium resources are sourced from salt lakes
     鋰礦。成熟的鹽湖主要分佈在南美鋰三                                            and hard rock lithium mines. Well-developed salt lakes are mainly
     角和中國，大部分鋰礦山依然集中在澳                                            found in the lithium delta of South America and in China, while the
     大利亞。近年來，在終端市場的需求刺                                            majority of lithium mines are concentrated in Australia. In recent years,
     激下，鋰資源的投資開發力度加大，供                                            stimulated by demand in the end-user market, there has been an
     應逐漸多元化。根據國投證券研究所的                                            increased investment and development in lithium resources, leading to
     數據，2024年預計全球鋰資源供應量                                           a diversification of supply. According to the data from SDIC Securities
     為129.41萬噸碳酸鋰當量（「LCE」），同                                      Research Institute, the global supply of lithium resource is expected
     比增長20%，其中鹽湖、鋰輝石及回收                                           to reach 1,294,100 tons of lithium carbonate equivalent (“LCE”) in
     來源分別是57.05萬噸LCE，68.49萬噸                                      2024, representing a year-on-year increase of 20%, among which
     LCE及3.87萬噸LCE，分別佔比44%，                                       570,500 tons of LCE, 684,900 tons of LCE and 38,700 tons of LCE
     53%及3%。                                                      are sourced from salt lakes, spodumene and recycling, accounting for
                                                                  44%, 53% and 3%, respectively.

     行業回顧（續）                                             INDUSTRY REVIEW (Continued)

     1.    鋰資源市場分析（續）                                    1.   Analysis of lithium resource market (Continued)

           (1)    鋰輝石精礦市場                                     (1)   Market of spodumene concentrate

                  澳大利亞是全球最大的鋰礦石生                                    Australia is one of the world’s largest producers of lithium ore,
                  產國之一，當地礦業發達、法律                                    with a well-developed mining industry, comprehensive laws and
                  法規齊全、基礎設施良好。根據                                    regulations, and good infrastructure. According to the data from
                  Fastmarkets的數據，截至2024年                            Fastmarkets, as of June 2024, the domestic CIF price of 5%–
                  6月，5%–6%鋰輝石精礦的中國                                  6% spodumene concentrate was around USD1,000–1,010 per
                  到岸價格約合1,000–1,010美元╱                              ton, representing a slight recovery from the price of USD900–
                  噸，較2024年初價格900–1,000                              1,000 per ton at the beginning of 2024. In Australia, being an
                  美元╱噸略微回升。澳大利亞作                                    important source of global lithium resources, the supply of
                  為全球鋰礦資源的重要產地， 主                                   lithium ore from major producers is relatively stable, while the
                  要生產商的鋰礦供應量相對穩                                     expansion and resumption of production of some lithium mining
                  定， 同時2024年部分鋰礦項目的                                 projects and the gradual release of capacity of new lithium
                  擴建及復產、新的鋰礦項目產能                                    mining projects in 2024 are all incremental to the supply of
                  逐漸釋放均為鋰輝石供給帶來增                                    lithium pyroxene. The African continent has abundant resources
                  量。 非洲大陸擁有豐富的鋰輝石                                   of spodumene and petalite, with high ore grades. However,
                  和透鋰長石資源，礦石品位高，                                    the overall development progress is slow due to insufficient
                  但由於勘探投入不夠以及配套基                                    investment in exploration and relatively backwards supporting
                  礎設施相對落後的因素，整體開                                    infrastructure, and the mature mines in production are primarily
                  發速度緩慢，截至目前在產成熟                                    concentrated in Zimbabwe so far. Over the past two years, the
                  的礦山主要分佈在津巴布韋。過                                    lithium mine projects of the African continent have increasingly
                  去兩年，非洲大陸的鋰礦項目憑                                    attracted Chinese companies due to the favorable investment
                  藉友好的投資環境對中資企業的                                    environment. The incremental capacity of spodumene projects
                  吸引力不斷增強。2024年非洲的                                  in Africa in 2024 will mainly come from the ramp-up capacity
                  鋰輝石項目產能增量主要來自於                                    from mines that have been or are to be put into operation. With

5.   儲能市場分析                          5.      Analysis of the energy storage market

     隨著全球對碳排放的關注加劇，以及碳                       With the increasing global focus on carbon emissions and the
     中和策略的不斷加強，傳統的化石燃料                       strengthening of carbon neutrality strategies, the traditional fossil fuel
     能源體系正迅速向以清潔和低碳可再生                       energy system is rapidly transforming towards a structure with clean,
     能源為核心的結構轉型。在此大背景                        low-carbon, and renewable energy sources as the core. Against this

     6.    動力電池回收利用市場分析                                  6.   Analysis of the power battery recycling market

           動力電池作為電動汽車的關鍵部件之                                   As one of the key components of electric vehicles, motive power
           一，隨著新能源汽車產業的高速發展得                                  batteries have been widely used with the rapid development of the
           到了廣泛應用。隨著動力電池將進入                                   new energy vehicle industry. As motive power batteries will enter
           大規模退役期，對動力電池開展回收                                   into a large-scale decommissioning period, it is important to carry
           利用已至關重要，引起了國家、社會                                   out the recycling of motive power batteries, which has drawn high
           的高度關注。《新能源汽車產業發展規                                  concerns from countries and societies. The New Energy Vehicle
           劃（2021–2035年）
                       》提出，要完善動力                              Industry Development Plan (2021–2035) (《新能源汽車產業發展規劃

           利用體系；加強動力電池全生命週期監                                  power battery recovery, cascade utilization and recycling; strengthen
           管；支持動力電池梯次產品在儲能、備                                  the supervision of the whole life cycle of motive power batteries;
           能、充換電等領域創新應用；加強餘能                                  support the innovative application of motive power battery cascade
           檢測、殘值評估、重組利用、安全管理                                  products in energy storage, energy reserve, charging and swapping;
           等技術研發。從佈局上看，產業鏈上下                                  and strengthen the research and development of residual energy
           游企業均在積極開展回收再利用佈局，                                  inspection, residual value evaluation, recombination utilization and
           隨著動力電池報廢高潮的臨近，對廢棄                                  safety management. From the perspective of layout, the upstream
           動力電池加以合理回收利用有極大的意                                  and downstream enterprises of the industrial chain have actively
           義和必要性；從應用領域看，退役動力                                  carried out the recycling layout. With the approaching of the scrapped

           大的應用潛力。根據國投證券推算，                                   to reasonably recycle the scrapped power batteries. From the

                  坪項目資源儲量LCE數據為總孔隙度資                                resource, and the LCE data for the Qinghai Yiliping project are
                  源儲量所含氯化鋰數據換算得出；3）持                                converted from the lithium chloride data contained in the total porosity
                  股比例為通過股權比例折算到項目持                                  resource reserve; 3) The ownership interest is converted to the project

     同時，公司在高安全長循環新型磷酸鐵          and development to realize the development, installation and

(1)   會計處理方法                       (1)     Accounting Treatment

      按照《國際財務報告準則第2號－股份支                   In accordance with the requirements of the IFRS 2 – Share-based
      付》的要求，公司將在歸屬期的每個資                    Payment, the Company shall, on each balance sheet date during the
      產負債表日，根據最新取得的受限制股                    vesting period, adjust the estimated number of Shares as an award
      份單位計劃選定參與者變動、業績指標                    that may be exercised in accordance with the latest number of the
      完成情況等後續信息，修正受限制股份                    RSU Selected Participants who have fulfilled the exercise conditions
      單位計劃的獎勵數量，並按照獎勵授予                    and the performance indicators, and recognize the services received
      日的公允價值，將當期取得的服務計入                    during the period as the relevant costs or expenses and capital
      相關成本或費用和資本公積。                        reserve in accordance with the fair value of the Shares as an award
                                           on the grant date.

      方法

      根據《國際財務報告準則第2號－股份支                   According to the relevant provisions on the determination of fair
      付》中關於公允價值確定的相關規定，                    value in the IFRS 2 – Share-based Payment, an appropriate valuation
      需要選擇適當的估值模型對受限制股份                    model is required to be selected to calculate the fair value under RSU
      單位計劃下的公允價值進行計算。公司                    Scheme. The Company chooses Black-Scholes model (B-S model) to
      選擇Black-Scholes模型（B-S模型）來計           calculate the fair value, and then uses the model to calculate the total
      算該公允價值，其後使用該模型計算                     fair value to be HKD11,720,117.22 as at the grant date.

(1)   會計處理方法                      (1)      Accounting Treatment

      按照《國際財務報告準則第2號－股份支                   According to the relevant provisions on the determination of fair value

      屬期內的服務或達到規定業績條件才可                    exchange for the staff services and settled with equity that cannot be
      行權的換取職工服務的以權益結算的股                    exercised only until the service in waiting period is completed or the
      份支付，在歸屬期內的每個資產負債表                    performance condition required is realized, the service obtained in
      日，應當以對可行權權益工具數量的最                    the current period shall be included in relevant costs or expenses and
      佳估計為基礎，按照權益工具授予日的                    capital reserves on each balance sheet date within the waiting period,
      公允價值，將當期取得的服務計入相關                    on the basis of the best estimate of the number of vesting equity
      成本或費用和資本公積。                          instruments, as per the fair value of equity instruments of the grant

     遵守《企業管治守則》                                          COMPLIANCE WITH THE CORPORATE
                                                         GOVERNANCE CODE
     偏離《企業管治守則》第二部分的守則條文第                                Deviation from Code Provision B.2.2 in Part 2 of the Corporate
     B.2.2條                                              Governance Code

     《企業管治守則》第二部分的守則條文第B.2.2                             Code provision B.2.2 in part 2 of the Corporate Governance Code states
     條：每名董事（包括有指定任期的董事）應輪流                               that every Director, including those appointed for a specific term, should be
     退任，至少每三年一次。                                         subject to retirement by rotation at least once every three years.

     本公司第五屆董事會、本公司監事會（   「監事                             The three-year term of the fifth session of the Board and the supervisory
      ）的三年任期於2023年3月24日屆滿，鑒於
     會」                                                  committee of the Company (the “Supervisory Committee”) expired on
     新一屆相關董事會及監事會成員候選人的提名                                March 24, 2023. As the nomination of relevant candidates for the members
     工作尚未結束，部分候選人的適當性仍在評估                                of the new session of the Board and Supervisory Committee has not yet
     階段，為保持董事會、監事會工作的連續性，                                been completed, and the suitability of some of the candidates is still being
     董事會、監事會需要延期換屆選舉。同時，本                                assessed, the election and appointment of the members of the Board and
     公司第五屆董事會專門委員會和高級管理人員                                the Supervisory Committee will be postponed to maintain the continuity
     的任期亦相應順延。董事會認為，延期換屆選                                of the work of the Board and the Supervisory Committee. Meanwhile, the
     舉不會影響本公司的日常運營，原因為：                                  terms of the special committees under the fifth session of the Board and the
                                                         senior management of the Company will be extended correspondingly. The
                                                         Board believe that the postponement of the election and appointment will
                                                         not affect the daily operations of the Company for the following reasons:

     (1)   董事及本公司監事之領導對本集團業務                             (1)   The leadership of the Directors and supervisors of the Company is
           之持續及穩定性尤其重要，在未確認合                                   particularly important to the continuity and stability of the Group’s
           適的候選人以繼任董事及本公司監事，                                   business. Maintaining the original membership structure is conducive
           維持原本的成員架構有利於企業的日常                                   to the stability of the daily operation of the enterprise until suitable
           經營的穩定性；                                             candidates is identified to succeed the Directors and supervisors of
                                                               the Company;

     (2)   當前董事會成員對本公司企業管治及業                             (2)   The current Board members have extensive experience in the corporate
           務方面具備豐富的經驗，具有不同專業                                   governance and business of the Company, with different professional
           背景，並在企業管理、技術開發、財務                                   backgrounds and expertise in corporate management, technology
           管理、戰略投資、人力資源管理等方面                                   development, financial management, strategic investment and human
           擁有專業知識，因此換屆選舉過程需審                                   resources management, etc. Therefore, the process of election
           慎考慮候選人的適當性；在對候選人完                                   and appointment needs to be carefully considered in terms of the
           成適當性評估前，維持原本的成員架構                                   suitability of the candidates; and until the completion of the suitability
           有利於本公司以最佳利益的方式作出經                                   assessment of the candidates, it is beneficial for the Company to
           營決策。                                                maintain the original membership structure in order to make business
                                                               decisions in the best interest of the Company;

     (3)   各獨立非執行董事已根據香港上市規則                             (3)   Each of the independent non-executive Directors has provided the
           第3.13條的規定，就其獨立性每年向本                                 Company with an annual confirmation of his/her independence in
           公司做出確認，本公司已收到該等董事                                   accordance with Rule 3.13 of the Hong Kong Listing Rules. The
           的年度確認，認為該等董事於二零二三                                   Company has received annual confirmations from these Directors that
           年度確屬獨立人士，目前的成員架構可                                   each of the independent non-executive Directors is an independent

           的權責制約。                                              of power and authority for corporate governance and internal control.

     為解決偏離《企業管治守則》第二部分的守則                                To address the deviation from code provision B.2.2 in part 2 of the

            闡述如下：

            (a)     國際財務報告準則第16號之修訂                          (a)   Amendments to IFRS 16 specify the requirements that a seller-
                    闡明計量售後回租交易產生的租                                 lessee uses in measuring the lease liability arising in a sale
                    賃負債所用的賣方－承租人之規                                 and leaseback transaction to ensure the seller-lessee does
                    定，以確保賣方－承租人不會確                                 not recognise any amount of the gain or loss that relates to
                    認與所保留使用權有關的任何損                                 the right of use it retains. Since the Group has no sale and
                    益金額。由於本集團自初始應用                                 leaseback transactions with variable lease payments that do
                    國際財務報告準則第16號之日起                                not depend on an index or a rate occurring from the date of
                    概無不取決於指數或利率的可變                                 initial application of IFRS 16, the amendments did not have any
                    租賃款項的售後租回交易，因此                                 impact on the financial position or performance of the Group.
                    該等修訂未對本集團的財務狀況
                    或業績產生任何影響。

            (b)     2020年之修訂澄清了將負債分                          (b)   The 2020 Amendments clarify the requirements for classifying
                    類為流動或非流動負債之規定，                                 liabilities as current or non-current, including what is meant by
                    包括延遲清償權利的含義，以及                                 a right to defer settlement and that a right to defer must exist
                    延遲清償權利於報告期末必須存                                 at the end of the reporting period. Classification of a liability is
                    在。負債之分類不受該實體行使                                 unaffected by the likelihood that the entity will exercise its right
                    其權利延遲清償之可能性影響。                                 to defer settlement. The amendments also clarify that a liability
                    該等修訂本亦闡明負債可以用其                                 can be settled in its own equity instruments, and that only if a
                    自身權益工具結算，只有當可轉                                 conversion option in a convertible liability is itself accounted for
                    換負債中的轉換選擇權本身作為                                 as an equity instrument would the terms of a liability not impact
                    權益工具入賬時，負債的條款才                                 its classification. The 2022 Amendments further clarify that,
                    不會影響其分類。2022年之修訂                               among covenants of a liability arising from a loan arrangement,
                    進一步闡明，在該等由貸款安排                                 only those with which an entity must comply on or before the
                    產生之負債契諾中，只有實體須                                 reporting date affect the classification of that liability as current

                    會影響該負債分類為流動或非流                                 current liabilities that are subject to the entity complying with
                    動。倘實體在報告期後12個月內                                future covenants within 12 months after the reporting period.

     出於管理目的，本集團根據其產品及服                For management purposes, the Group is organised into business

     營板塊：                             operating segments as follows:

           製造、銷售及提供加工服務；                    of lithium products, and rendering of processing services;

     (b)   鋰電池板塊：鋰電池製造及銷              (b)   Lithium battery segment: manufacture and sale of lithium
           售；及                              batteries; and

           其他礦產品等的勘探及銷售。                    of lithium ore, other lithium products and others.

     管理層對本集團各經營板塊的經營業績                Management monitors the results of the Group’s operating segments
     分開管理，以此決定資源分配及業績評                separately for the purpose of making decisions about resource
     估。板塊業績基於各項須予呈報的板塊                allocation and performance assessment. Segment performance
     溢利╱虧損進行評價，該板塊溢利╱虧                is evaluated based on reportable segment profit/loss, which is a
     損以經調整的除稅前溢利╱虧損進行計                measure of adjusted profit/loss before tax. The adjusted profit/loss
     量。該經調整的除稅前溢利╱虧損將利                before tax is measured consistently with the Group’s profit before tax
     息收入及與租賃無關的融資成本排除在                except that interest income and non-lease related finance costs are
     外。除此以外，其計量與本集團之除稅                excluded from such measurement.
     前溢利的計量相一致。

15. 業務合併                         15. BUSINESS COMBINATION
  於期內，本公司附屬公司青海良承礦業                     During the period, Qinghai Liangcheng Mining Co., Ltd., a subsidiary
  有限公司與相關第三方進行了一系列股                     of the Company, conducted a series of equity transactions with
  權交易，以金融資產和現金交易代價人                     relevant third parties and acquired Qinghai Fengjin Mining Co.,
  民幣2,788,561,000元收購青海鳳金礦               Ltd. and its subsidiaries, Kunming AVIC Phosphorus Chemical
  業有限公司及其附屬公司、昆明市中航                     Co., Ltd. and its subsidiaries, at a transaction consideration of
  燐化工有限公司及其附屬公司，以進一                     RMB2,788,561,000 with financial assets and cash, in order to further
  步完善本集團在礦產資源領域的佈局，                     improve the Group’s layout in mineral resources, which constituted a
  構成了一攬子交易的業務合併。                        business combination as a package deal.

  截至財務報表批准和發佈之日，對收購                     As of the date of approval and issuance of the financial statements,
  的相關資產和負債的評估尚未完成。因                     the evaluations of the relevant assets and liabilities acquired have
  此，只能暫時確定合併獲得的每項可識                     not yet been completed. Therefore, the fair value of each identifiable
  別資產、負債和或有負債的公允價值以                     asset, liability and contingent liability acquired in the combination
  及商譽金額。如果臨時確認的價值在                      and the amount of goodwill can only be determined temporarily. If
  2025年1月（收購日期後12個月）之前有                 the temporary value recognized is adjusted before January 2025 (12
  調整，則視為於收購日期確認和計量。                     months after the acquisition date), it is deemed to be recognized and
                                        measured on the acquisition date.

             本集團以財務經理為首的財務部門負責                                   The Group’s finance department headed by the finance manager is
             釐定金融工具之公允價值計量的政策及                                   responsible for determining the policies and procedures for the fair
             程序。財務經理直接向首席財務官及審                                   value measurement of financial instruments. The finance manager
             核委員會報告。在每個報告日，財務部                                   reports directly to the chief financial officer and the audit committee.
             門分析金融工具的價值變動並釐定在估                                   At each reporting date, the finance department analyses the

             年就中期及年度財務報告與審核委員會                                   approved by the chief financial officer. The valuation process and
             討論兩次。                                               results are discussed with the audit committee twice a year for interim
